The Pattern Ontology (PO)

http://purl.org/spar/po
bibliographic citation
Di Iorio, A., Peroni, S., Poggi, F., Vitali, F. (2012). Aﬁrst approach to the automatic recognition ofstructural patterns in XML documents. In Proceedingsof the 2012 ACM symposium on Document Engineering(DocEng 2012): 85-94. DOI: 10.1145/2361354.2361374
changes
* **v1.4.3**: Added new documentation, including annotation properties (e.g., `bibo:doi`, `schema:codeRepository`, etc.), term properties (e.g., `vann:example`, `vs:term_status`, etc.), exemplary competency questions with their respective SPARQL queries, and a Grafoo diagram.
code repository
https://github.com/SPAROntologies/po

Sebastian Barzaghi (https://orcid.org/0000-0002-0799-1527)

Angelo Di Iorio (https://orcid.org/0000-0002-6893-7452)
date
2026-09-21
description
The **_Pattern Ontology (PO)_** is an OWL 2 DL ontology that formally defines patterns for segmenting a document into atomic components, in order to be manipulated independently and re-flowed in different contexts. ![The main architecture of PO.](po.png) Structural patterns allow representing document elements (such as sections, paragraphs, lists, metadata, and tables) through abstract, reusable primitives independent of specific markup syntaxes. ## Examples of use In the following subsections, we introduce some examples to showcase how to use PO. The prefixes that are used in all the examples provided below are defined as follows: @prefix : <http://sparontologies.net/example/> . @prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> . @prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> . ### Structural decomposition of a document section With PO, it is possible to represent document elements and how they manifest as patterns occurring within the document's stucture itself. For example, the following example shows how to use PO to model a section defined as a headed container and containing a heading block as its header and a body paragraph block containing inline text and atom elements. :section-1 a po:HeadedContainer ; po:hasName "section" ; po:containsAsHeader :heading-1 ; po:contains :paragraph-1 . :heading-1 a po:Block ; po:hasName "h1" ; po:contains :text-heading . :paragraph-1 a po:Block ; po:hasName "p" ; po:contains :text-span . :text-span a po:Inline ; po:hasName "span" ; po:contains :atom-text . :text-heading a po:Atom ; po:hasName "text" . :atom-text a po:Atom ; po:hasName "text" . ## Competency Questions PO can be used for answering several questions related to the atomic components that form a document accoridng to certain patterns. In the following subsections, some of them are introduced together with their respective SPARQL queries. The prefixes that are used in all the SPARQL queries provided below are defined as follows: PREFIX po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> ### CQ1 Which header elements are contained within a headed container? SELECT ?header WHERE { ?section a po:headedContainer ; po:containsAsHeader ?header . } ### CQ2 Which block elements are directly contained within a container? SELECT ?block WHERE { ?section po:contains ?block . ?block a po:Block . }

label
atom element

example

term status
status stable
note
Similarly to `po:Inline`, elements following the 'atom' pattern can only be contained within block or inline elements (and consequently they also cannot be used as root elements of documents). It can contain textual content and no other elements.
en

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:Atom a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "atom element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-atom a po:Atom ;
                po:isContainedBy :my-paragraph .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "Literal text that is part of the document body."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ;
            owl:someValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                    owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Inline ) ] ] ;
    owl:disjointWith po:Field ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Flat [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                                owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Inline ) ] ;
                        owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable ;
    skos:note """
Similarly to `po:Inline`, elements following the 'atom' pattern can only be contained within block or inline elements (and consequently they also cannot be used as root elements of documents). 
It can contain textual content and no other elements.
    """@en .

block element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Block

comment
An element that organises the document content as a sequence of nestable elements and text nodes.
en
is disjoint with
inline elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is equivalent to
(
mixed elementc
and
is contained byop
only (
container elementc
or
popup elementc
) )
is sub concept of
containsop
only (
atom elementc
or
inline elementc
or
milestone elementc
or
popup elementc
)
label
block element
en
example
:my-paragraph a po:Block ; po:isContainedBy :my-section ; po:contains :my-span .
term status
status stable
note
In particular, elements following this pattern can contain text and other elements compliant with the patterns `po:Inline`, `po:Atom`, `po:Milestones` and `po:Popup`. Furthermore, it is a requirement that block elements are contained only by container and popup elements.
en

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:Block a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "block element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-paragraph a po:Block ;
                po:isContainedBy :my-section ;
                po:contains :my-span .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element that organises the document content as a sequence of nestable elements and text nodes."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                    owl:unionOf ( po:Atom po:Inline po:Milestone po:Popup ) ] ;
            owl:onProperty po:contains ] ;
    owl:disjointWith po:Inline ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Mixed [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                                owl:unionOf ( po:Container po:Popup ) ] ;
                        owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable ;
    skos:note """
In particular, elements following this pattern can contain text and other elements compliant with the patterns `po:Inline`, `po:Atom`, `po:Milestones` and `po:Popup`. 

Furthermore, it is a requirement that block elements are contained only by container and popup elements.
    """@en .

bucket element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Bucket

comment
An element that can contain other elements but no text nodes.
en
is disjoint with
marker elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is sub concept of
non-textual elementc,
structured elementc
label
bucket element
en
term status
status stable
example
:my-bucket a po:Bucket ; po:contains :item-1, :item-2 . 

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:Bucket a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "bucket element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-bucket a po:Bucket ;
                po:contains :item-1, :item-2 .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element that can contain other elements but no text nodes."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf po:NonTextual,
        po:Structured ;
    owl:disjointWith po:Marker ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable .

container element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Container

comment
An element concerning the structural organization of a document.
en
is disjoint with
popup elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is equivalent to
(
bucket elementc
and
is contained byop
only (
container elementc
or
popup elementc
) )
is sub concept of
containsop
only (
block elementc
or
container elementc
or
field elementc
or
meta elementc
)
label
container element
en
note
Elements following this pattern contain no textual content and contain only elements compliant with the patterns: `po:Meta`, `po:Field`, `po:Block` and any subtype of `po:Container`. It is disjointed with the pattern `po:Popup`, although they share the same content model.
en
example
:my-body a po:Container ; po:contains :section-1, :section-2 .
term status
status stable

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:Container a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "container element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-body a po:Container ;
                po:contains :section-1, :section-2 .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element concerning the structural organization of a document."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                    owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Container po:Field po:Meta ) ] ;
            owl:onProperty po:contains ] ;
    owl:disjointWith po:Popup ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Bucket [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                                owl:unionOf ( po:Container po:Popup ) ] ;
                        owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable ;
    skos:note """
Elements following this pattern contain no textual content and contain only elements compliant with the patterns: `po:Meta`, `po:Field`, `po:Block` and any subtype of `po:Container`. 

It is disjointed with the pattern `po:Popup`, although they share the same content model.
    """@en .

field element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Field

comment
Literal metadata or text that is not really part of the document body.
en
is disjoint with
atom elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is equivalent to
(
flat elementc
and
is contained byop
only (
container elementc
or
popup elementc
) )
label
field element
en
example
:my-author-name a po:Field ; po:isContainedBy :my-author-header .
term status
status stable
note
Its main difference with `po:Meta` is that `po:Field` can contain textual content.
en

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:Field a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "field element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-author-name a po:Field ;
                po:isContainedBy :my-author-header .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "Literal metadata or text that is not really part of the document body."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Flat [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                                owl:unionOf ( po:Container po:Popup ) ] ;
                        owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable ;
    skos:note """
Its main difference with `po:Meta` is that `po:Field` can contain textual content.
    """@en .

flat element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Flat

comment
An element that can contain text nodes but no elements.
en
is disjoint with
mixed elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is sub concept of
non-structured elementc,
textual elementc
label
flat element
en
term status
status stable
example
:my-flat a po:Flat ; po:hasName "text" . 

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:Flat a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "flat element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-flat a po:Flat ;
                po:hasName "text" .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element that can contain text nodes but no elements."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf po:NonStructured,
        po:Textual ;
    owl:disjointWith po:Mixed ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable .

po:HeadedContainer a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "headed-container element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-section a po:HeadedContainer ;
                po:containsAsHeader :my-title ;
                po:contains :my-para .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "A container element whose content model needs to begin with one or more block elements (the heading)."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Container [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:allValuesFrom po:Block ;
                        owl:onProperty po:containsAsHeader ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable .

[] a owl:AllDisjointClasses ;
    owl:members ( po:HeadedContainer po:Record po:Table ) .

inline element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Inline

comment
An element that organises the document content as a sequence of nestable elements and text nodes, and that can contain other elements compliant with the same pattern.
en
is disjoint with
block elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is equivalent to
(
mixed elementc
and
is contained byop
only (
block elementc
or
inline elementc
) )
is sub concept of
containsop
only (
atom elementc
or
inline elementc
or
milestone elementc
or
popup elementc
)
is contained byop
some (
block elementc
or
inline elementc
)
label
inline element
en
term status
status stable
note
This class has the same use and content model of the pattern Block, but differing primarily because a) inline elements can contain other elements compliant with the same pattern while block elements cannot, and b) inline elements must always be contained by other block or inline elements and by no other type of element. These constraints also imply that inline elements cannot be used as root elements of documents and that the class Block is disjoint with the class Inline (i.e., a markup element cannot be a block and an inline at the same time).
en
example
:my-emphasis a po:Inline ; po:isContainedBy :my-paragraph ; po:contains :my-atom-text . 

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:Inline a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "inline element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-emphasis a po:Inline ;
                po:isContainedBy :my-paragraph ;
                po:contains :my-atom-text .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element that organises the document content as a sequence of nestable elements and text nodes, and that can contain other elements compliant with the same pattern."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ;
            owl:someValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                    owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Inline ) ] ],
        [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                    owl:unionOf ( po:Atom po:Inline po:Milestone po:Popup ) ] ;
            owl:onProperty po:contains ] ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Mixed [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                                owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Inline ) ] ;
                        owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable ;
    skos:note """
This class has the same use and content model of the pattern Block, but differing primarily because a) inline elements can contain other elements compliant with the same pattern while block elements cannot, and b) inline elements must always be contained by other block or inline elements and by no other type of element.

These constraints also imply that inline elements cannot be used as root elements of documents and that the class Block is disjoint with the class Inline (i.e., a markup element cannot be a block and an inline at the same time)."""@en .

This class has the same use and content model of the pattern Block, but differing primarily because a) inline elements can contain other elements compliant with the same pattern while block elements cannot, and b) inline elements must always be contained by other block or inline elements and by no other type of element.

marker element

comment
An element that can contain neither text nodes nor elements.
en

bucket elementc

is sub concept of

non-textual elementc

marker element
en
example

term status
status stable

po:Marker a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "marker element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-break-tag a po:Marker ;
                po:hasName "br" .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element that can contain neither text nodes nor elements."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;

An element that contains neither other elements nor textual content.

label
meta element
en
example
:my-doc-meta a po:Meta ; po:isContainedBy :my-doc-header .
note
Contrarily to the pattern `po:Milestone`, which was meant to describe markup elements that impact the document because of their location, the main feature of its disjoint sibling is the mere existence, independently from the position it has within the document. `po:Meta` elements convey metadata information about the document or part of it, independently of where they are. Thus, meta elements can be contained only within container elements.
en

status stable

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .

po:Meta a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "meta element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-doc-meta a po:Meta ;
                po:isContainedBy :my-doc-header .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element that contains neither other elements nor textual content."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    owl:disjointWith po:Milestone ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Marker [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ;
                        owl:someValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                                owl:unionOf ( po:Container po:Popup ) ] ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable ;
    skos:note """
Contrarily to the pattern `po:Milestone`, which was meant to describe markup elements that impact the document because of their location, the main feature of its disjoint sibling is the mere existence, independently from the position it has within the document. 
`po:Meta` elements convey metadata information about the document or part of it, independently of where they are. 
Thus, meta elements can be contained only within container elements.
    """@en .

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .

po:Milestone a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "milestone element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-page-break a po:Milestone ;
                po:isContainedBy :my-paragraph .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment """
An elements that contains neither other elements nor textual content.
"""@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ;
            owl:someValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                    owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Inline ) ] ;
            skos:note """
Similarly to `po:Inline`, elements following the `po:Milestone` pattern can only be contained within block or inline elements (and consequently they also cannot be used as root elements of documents).

The distinctive characteristic of the pattern `po:Milestone` is the location it assumes within the document.
    """@en ] ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Marker [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                                owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Inline ) ] ;
                        owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable .

mixed element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Mixed

comment
An element that can contain other elements and text nodes.
en
is disjoint with
flat elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is sub concept of
structured elementc,
textual elementc
label
mixed element
en
example
:my-paragraph a po:Mixed ; po:contains :link .
term status
status stable

po:Mixed a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "mixed element"@en ;
    vann:example """

    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;

        po:Textual ;

non-structured element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#NonStructured

comment
An element that cannot contain other elements.
en
is disjoint with
structured elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is equivalent to
(
structured elementc
)
is sub concept of
pattern-based elementc
label
non-structured element
en
term status
status stable
example
:my-leaf-node a po:NonStructured . 

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:NonStructured a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "non-structured element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-leaf-node a po:NonStructured .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element that cannot contain other elements."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf po:Pattern ;
    owl:disjointWith po:Structured ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:complementOf po:Structured ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable .

popup element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Popup

comment
A bucket element that is only present within block and inline elements.
en
is disjoint with
container elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is equivalent to
(
bucket elementc
and
is contained byop
only (
block elementc
or
inline elementc
) )
is sub concept of
is contained byop
some (
block elementc
or
inline elementc
)
containsop
only (
block elementc
or
container elementc
or
field elementc
or
meta elementc
)
label
popup element
en
note
Popup elements are used whenever complex structures need to be placed within content elements such as paragraphs.
en
term status
status stable
example
:my-footnote a po:Popup ; po:isContainedBy :my-paragraph ; po:contains :my-footnote-block . 

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .

po:Popup a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "popup element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-footnote a po:Popup ;
                po:isContainedBy :my-paragraph ;
                po:contains :my-footnote-block .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "A bucket element that is only present within block and inline elements."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ;
            owl:someValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                    owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Inline ) ] ],
        [ a owl:Restriction ;
            owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                    owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Container po:Field po:Meta ) ] ;
            owl:onProperty po:contains ] ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Bucket [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:allValuesFrom [ a owl:Class ;
                                owl:unionOf ( po:Block po:Inline ) ] ;
                        owl:onProperty po:isContainedBy ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable ;
    skos:note """
Popup elements are used whenever complex structures need to be placed within content elements such as paragraphs.
    """@en .

record element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Record

comment
A container element that can only contain heterogeneous and non repeatable elements.
en
is disjoint with
headed-container elementc,
table elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is equivalent to
(
container elementc
and
can contain heteronymous elementsdp
value true and
can contain homonymous elementsdp
value false )
label
record element
en
example
:my-author-record a po:Record ; po:canContainHeteronymousElements true ; po:canContainHomonymousElements false .
term status
status stable

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .

@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

po:Record a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "record element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-author-record a po:Record ;
                po:canContainHeteronymousElements true ;
                po:canContainHomonymousElements false .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "A container element that can only contain heterogeneous and non repeatable elements."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Container [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:hasValue true ;
                        owl:onProperty po:canContainHeteronymousElements ] [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:hasValue false ;
                        owl:onProperty po:canContainHomonymousElements ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable .

[] a owl:AllDisjointClasses ;
    owl:members ( po:HeadedContainer po:Record po:Table ) .

structured element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Structured

comment
An element that can contain other elements.
en
is disjoint with
non-structured elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is in domain of
can contain heteronymous elementsdp,
can contain homonymous elementsdp,
containsop
is sub concept of
pattern-based elementc
label
structured element
en
example
:my-section a po:Structured ; po:contains :my-paragraph .
term status
status stable

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .

po:Structured a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "structured element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-section a po:Structured ;
                po:contains :my-paragraph .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "An element that can contain other elements."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    rdfs:subClassOf po:Pattern ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable .

table element
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#Table

comment
A container element that must contain only homogeneous elements (but they can be repeated).
en
is disjoint with
headed-container elementc,
record elementc
is defined by
http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#
is equivalent to
(
container elementc
and
can contain heteronymous elementsdp
value false and
can contain homonymous elementsdp
value true )
label
table element
en
term status
status stable
example
:my-list a po:Table ; po:canContainHeteronymousElements false ; po:canContainHomonymousElements true . 

@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix po: <http://www.essepuntato.it/2008/12/pattern#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix reg: <http://purl.org/linked-data/registry#> .
@prefix vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/> .
@prefix vs: <http://www.w3.org/2003/06/sw-vocab-status/ns#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

po:Table a owl:Class ;
    rdfs:label "table element"@en ;
    vann:example """
        :my-list a po:Table ;
                po:canContainHeteronymousElements false ;
                po:canContainHomonymousElements true .
        """ ;
    rdfs:comment "A container element that must contain only homogeneous elements (but they can be repeated)."@en ;
    rdfs:isDefinedBy po: ;
    owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ;
            owl:intersectionOf ( po:Container [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:hasValue false ;
                        owl:onProperty po:canContainHeteronymousElements ] [ a owl:Restriction ;
                        owl:hasValue true ;
                        owl:onProperty po:canContainHomonymousElements ] ) ] ;
    vs:term_status reg:statusStable .

[] a owl:AllDisjointClasses ;
    owl:members ( po:HeadedContainer po:Record po:Table ) .
